3. Story first, then operational chunks

GPT-6.1 Sol

Step 1 · Story

The city is almost asleep. Rain glistens on the pavement, shop shutters are pulled down, and a tiny corner store spills fluorescent light onto an otherwise empty street.

A young adult in an oversized charcoal jacket steps outside, carrying a paper cup. Their white sneakers are spotless against the wet asphalt. They pause beside a puddle reflecting a flickering pink sign.

Then the reflection flickers differently.

A bright pink fish swims through the reflected letters. The person crouches, curious, and touches the water with one fingertip. A ripple spreads beyond the puddle, rolling across the pavement like liquid light.

Pink becomes violet, violet becomes electric blue. Color climbs the brick walls. Painted flowers bloom across the shutters, and the white stripes of a pedestrian crossing lift gently from the road, becoming a floating staircase.

The person smiles, sets down their cup, and climbs.

Above the rooftops, the sleeping city has become a luminous playground. Tiny constellations drift between chimneys. Laundry sways in an unfelt breeze, each shirt glowing a different color. The person reaches the highest step and sits, sneakers dangling over the sparkling streets.

The corner-store sign clicks off.

Everything settles back into place. The person stands beside the ordinary puddle again. They retrieve their cup and start walking home.

Only their shoelaces have changed: one pink, one electric blue, leaving faint sparks with every step.

Step 2 · Operational chunks

Production plan

Format: A 60-second Instagram Reel in 15 sequential chunks. No added narration, dialogue, or captions. The “OPEN” wording on the store sign is a production choice; it provides consistent lettering for the reflection.

Chunk 1 — City Almost Asleep
  • Duration: 4 seconds
  • Visual: Slow street-level push toward a tiny corner store. Wet pavement glistens; neighboring shop shutters are closed. Fluorescent light spills from the store onto the empty street.
  • On-screen text: “OPEN” on the small pink corner-store sign. No overlays.
  • Audio: No voice. Quiet nighttime city ambience, occasional water drips, fluorescent hum. A restrained ambient-electronic score begins.
  • Entities: Corner store with a pink “OPEN” sign; closed metal shutters; wet brick buildings; nearby puddle and pedestrian crossing. Maintain this street layout throughout.
  • Environment: Late night, almost deserted urban street. Cool charcoal and blue-gray palette, fluorescent white light, isolated pink accent.
  • Depends on: None.
  • Source: “The city is almost asleep. Rain glistens on the pavement, shop shutters are pulled down, and a tiny corner store spills fluorescent light onto an otherwise empty street.”
Chunk 2 — Stepping Outside
  • Duration: 4 seconds
  • Visual: The young adult exits the store carrying a paper cup in their left hand. Track downward briefly to show spotless white sneakers against wet asphalt.
  • On-screen text: “OPEN” on the store sign when visible. No overlays.
  • Audio: No voice. Door movement, soft footsteps on wet ground, continuing fluorescent hum and score.
  • Entities: Person: young adult, medium-brown skin, short dark curls, oversized charcoal jacket, black trousers, spotless white sneakers with white shoelaces. Plain kraft-paper cup with an off-white lid.
  • Environment: Same street and lighting. Store light edges the jacket and shoes.
  • Depends on: Chunk 1 for store appearance, street geography, lighting, and sign.
  • Source: “A young adult in an oversized charcoal jacket steps outside, carrying a paper cup. Their white sneakers are spotless against the wet asphalt.”
Chunk 3 — An Unusual Reflection
  • Duration: 3 seconds
  • Visual: The person stops beside the puddle. Over-the-shoulder framing reveals the pink sign reflected in the water. Its reflection briefly flickers out of rhythm with the physical sign.
  • On-screen text: “OPEN,” mirrored in the puddle; the physical sign may appear above it. No overlays.
  • Audio: No voice. Score thins momentarily. Electrical flicker, a faint irregular synthetic pulse.
  • Entities: Same person and cup, still held in the left hand. Same puddle and pink sign; distinguish the ordinary sign flicker from the anomalous reflection.
  • Environment: Cool nighttime street; pink reflected light dominates the puddle.
  • Depends on: Chunks 1–2 for geography, person identity, wardrobe, and cup.
  • Source: “They pause beside a puddle reflecting a flickering pink sign. Then the reflection flickers differently.”
Chunk 4 — Fish in the Letters
  • Duration: 4 seconds
  • Visual: Close-up of the puddle. A bright pink fish swims through the reflected letters, weaving between them. Cut or tilt to the person’s curious expression as they crouch.
  • On-screen text: Mirrored “OPEN” within the reflection. No overlays.
  • Audio: No voice. A delicate watery shimmer joins the score; subtle jacket movement as the person crouches.
  • Entities: Same person, holding the cup upright in their left hand. One luminous pink fish exists only within the reflection, never above the water.
  • Environment: Same puddle location. Pink illumination softly reaches the person’s face.
  • Depends on: Chunk 3 for puddle framing, reflected lettering, and person position.
  • Source: “A bright pink fish swims through the reflected letters. The person crouches, curious, and touches the water with one fingertip.”
Chunk 5 — Touch and Ripple
  • Duration: 4 seconds
  • Visual: Tight shot of the person’s right index fingertip touching the water. A luminous ripple forms, crosses the puddle’s edge, and rolls across the wet pavement. Pull back enough to reveal that the ripple is no longer confined to water.
  • On-screen text: None; frame away from readable signage.
  • Audio: No voice. Small water-contact sound, followed by an expanding liquid-light whoosh. The score begins to swell.
  • Entities: Same person and cup. Right index finger creates the ripple; cup remains in the left hand. Ripple begins pink.
  • Environment: Existing street geometry remains unchanged as luminous energy spreads over it.
  • Depends on: Chunk 4’s crouched pose and hand positions; Chunk 3’s puddle location.
  • Source: “The person crouches, curious, and touches the water with one fingertip. A ripple spreads beyond the puddle, rolling across the pavement like liquid light.”
Chunk 6 — Color Climbs the City
  • Duration: 5 seconds
  • Visual: Follow the traveling light as pink becomes violet, then electric blue. Color climbs the brick walls. Flat, painted flower motifs unfurl across the closed shutters.
  • On-screen text: None.
  • Audio: No voice. Rising synth layers, soft blooming tones, and a shimmering sweep moving with the light.
  • Entities: Same brick walls and shutters. Flowers remain painted graphics on the shutters, not physical plants. Preserve the ordered transition: pink → violet → electric blue.
  • Environment: Nighttime street transitions from muted realism into luminous color while retaining its original architecture.
  • Depends on: Chunk 5’s outward-moving ripple; Chunk 1’s building and shutter designs.
  • Source: “Pink becomes violet, violet becomes electric blue. Color climbs the brick walls. Painted flowers bloom across the shutters…”
Chunk 7 — A Crossing Becomes Stairs
  • Duration: 5 seconds
  • Visual: Low-angle view of the pedestrian crossing. Its existing white stripes lift gently from the road, one after another, forming an ascending floating staircase toward the rooftops.
  • On-screen text: None.
  • Audio: No voice. Airy lift sounds and soft resonant pulses synchronized to each rising stripe. Music gains a gentle beat.
  • Entities: The established pedestrian crossing. Each step retains the white rectangular form of an original crossing stripe; no added railings or supports.
  • Environment: Same transformed street, with pink, violet, and electric-blue light reflected on wet asphalt.
  • Depends on: Chunk 1’s crossing placement and Chunk 6’s completed color transformation.
  • Source: “…and the white stripes of a pedestrian crossing lift gently from the road, becoming a floating staircase.”
Chunk 8 — Leaving the Cup Behind
  • Duration: 4 seconds
  • Visual: The person rises, smiles, and places the cup upright on the sidewalk beside the puddle. They step onto the first floating stripe and begin climbing.
  • On-screen text: None.
  • Audio: No voice. Gentle cup-placement tap, sneaker contact with the first step, brighter musical lift.
  • Entities: Same person and unchanged wardrobe. White shoelaces remain white. The same cup is left at a clearly established retrieval position beside the puddle.
  • Environment: Glowing street and floating staircase; original store remains below.
  • Depends on: Chunk 7’s staircase position; Chunks 4–5’s person and cup continuity.
  • Source: “The person smiles, sets down their cup, and climbs.”
Chunk 9 — Above the Rooftops
  • Duration: 4 seconds
  • Visual: Follow behind the person as they climb above roof level. Reveal the sleeping city transformed into a luminous playground, with sparkling streets below and small constellations drifting between chimneys.
  • On-screen text: None.
  • Audio: No voice. Airy rooftop ambience, delicate star-like chimes, expanding electronic score.
  • Entities: Same person and floating crossing-stripe stairs. Existing rooftops and chimneys; tiny floating constellation clusters.
  • Environment: Night sky above the same neighborhood. Luminous pink, violet, and electric-blue accents; city geometry remains recognizable.
  • Depends on: Chunk 8’s climbing motion and Chunk 7’s stair geometry.
  • Source: “Above the rooftops, the sleeping city has become a luminous playground. Tiny constellations drift between chimneys.”
Chunk 10 — Glowing Laundry
  • Duration: 5 seconds
  • Visual: Drift past a rooftop clothesline. Shirts sway gently despite the otherwise still air, each glowing a different color. Tiny constellations move between chimneys in the background; the person continues climbing farther back in frame.
  • On-screen text: None.
  • Audio: No voice. Quiet fabric rustle, small bell-like accents, continuing beat and ambient synths.
  • Entities: Same person, staircase, chimneys, and constellations. Clothesline with distinct shirts; assign each shirt a fixed glow color and retain it until the reset.
  • Environment: Rooftop nightscape. Still surrounding air; only the laundry exhibits the unfelt breeze.
  • Depends on: Chunk 9’s rooftop layout, constellation design, and climbing direction.
  • Source: “Tiny constellations drift between chimneys. Laundry sways in an unfelt breeze, each shirt glowing a different color.”
Chunk 11 — The Highest Step
  • Duration: 5 seconds
  • Visual: The person reaches the highest stripe and sits. Start with a side view of their sneakers dangling, then widen to reveal the sparkling streets below. Hold on their quiet delight.
  • On-screen text: None.
  • Audio: No voice. Music reaches its warm, luminous peak. Soft fabric movement and faint city ambience underneath.
  • Entities: Same person and highest floating stripe. Spotless white sneakers still have white shoelaces. Cup remains below beside the puddle.
  • Environment: Above the rooftops, surrounded by the established luminous city.
  • Depends on: Chunks 9–10 for rooftop geography and magical elements; Chunk 8 for cup location.
  • Source: “The person reaches the highest step and sits, sneakers dangling over the sparkling streets.”
Chunk 12 — The Sign Clicks Off
  • Duration: 2 seconds
  • Visual: Cut to the corner-store sign. It gives one final flicker and switches off.
  • On-screen text: “OPEN” before the switch-off; afterward, the same letters remain unlit. No overlays.
  • Audio: No voice. A distinct electrical click. Musical peak abruptly drains into a soft sustained tail.
  • Entities: The original pink “OPEN” sign. Do not introduce a switch operator or imply that the person turns it off.
  • Environment: Original corner-store exterior. Interior fluorescent spill remains; only the sign switches off.
  • Depends on: Chunks 1–3 for exact sign design and location.
  • Source: “The corner-store sign clicks off.”
Chunk 13 — Everything Settles
  • Duration: 4 seconds
  • Visual: Magical color recedes. Painted flowers disappear, constellations fade, shirts return to ordinary fabric, and crossing stripes settle back onto the road. Use a gentle visual match transition to reveal the person standing beside the ordinary puddle again, without a fall.
  • On-screen text: None; keep the unlit sign outside the main framing.
  • Audio: No voice. Soft descending shimmer, settling sounds, then ordinary nighttime ambience. Music becomes minimal.
  • Entities: Same person, restored street, ordinary puddle, and cup still upright at its established location. Reflected fish is gone. Keep shoelaces indistinct until the final reveal.
  • Environment: Return to the initial charcoal and blue-gray nighttime palette, with fluorescent store light. Pink sign stays off.
  • Depends on: Chunk 12’s switch-off; Chunks 6–11 for the elements being reset; Chunk 8 for cup placement.
  • Source: “Everything settles back into place. The person stands beside the ordinary puddle again.”
Chunk 14 — Walking Home
  • Duration: 3 seconds
  • Visual: The person retrieves the cup with their left hand and starts walking away from the store. Frame above shoe level to reserve the lace reveal.
  • On-screen text: None.
  • Audio: No voice. Cup pickup, natural footsteps, subdued score with a small returning melodic motif.
  • Entities: Same person, jacket, trousers, and cup. Store, puddle, and crossing are ordinary again.
  • Environment: Same quiet wet street, late-night fluorescent spill fading behind the person.
  • Depends on: Chunk 13’s standing position and reset environment; Chunk 8’s precise cup location.
  • Source: “They retrieve their cup and start walking home.”
Chunk 15 — A Little Magic Remains
  • Duration: 4 seconds
  • Visual: Low tracking close-up of the walking sneakers. Reveal a pink lace on the left shoe and an electric-blue lace on the right. Each footstep leaves faint, short-lived sparks over the wet pavement. End as the shoes move farther into the dark street.
  • On-screen text: None.
  • Audio: No voice. Wet-ground footsteps with delicate spark crackles. A final shimmering musical accent resolves the score.
  • Entities: Same spotless white sneakers. Only the shoelaces have changed: left pink, right electric blue. Sparks are subtle and temporary; they do not transform the city again.
  • Environment: Ordinary nighttime street in the original muted palette; localized lace color and faint sparks provide the only remaining magic.
  • Depends on: Chunk 14’s walking direction and pace; Chunk 2’s original sneaker design; Chunk 13’s restored environment.
  • Source: “Only their shoelaces have changed: one pink, one electric blue, leaving faint sparks with every step.”
Global requirements
  • Total duration: 60 seconds; chunk durations sum exactly to 60.
  • Aspect ratio: Proposed Instagram Reel format: 9:16 vertical, delivered at 1080 × 1920, 24 fps. Keep important action clear of interface-covered edges.
  • Identity: Use one original, consistently recognizable young adult throughout. Lock face, skin tone, hair, body proportions, jacket, trousers, and sneaker design before generating shots. No uploaded identity reference is available.
  • Object continuity: Maintain the same cup, store, sign, puddle, crossing, shutters, and street layout. Cup stays beside the puddle during the rooftop sequence. Shoelaces remain white before the reset, then pink on the left and electric blue on the right.
  • Music: One continuous, original or appropriately licensed ambient-electronic track. Progress from subdued nighttime atmosphere to wonder, peak above the rooftops, fall away when the sign clicks off, and resolve softly at the lace reveal.
  • Voice: No narration or dialogue. Tell the story through visuals, music, and sound effects.
  • Style: Cinematic magical realism: believable materials, wet reflections, natural human movement, restrained luminous effects. The magical city must remain recognizably the original city.
  • Color: Begin muted and cool. Introduce magic in the exact order pink → violet → electric blue. Restore the ordinary palette before revealing the changed shoelaces.
  • Text: No captions, titles, calls to action, watermarks, or added branding. The only readable text is the diegetic “OPEN” sign and its mirrored reflection.
  • Editing and continuity: Match action, camera direction, object positions, and light between adjacent chunks. Use character, location, and prop reference frames throughout. The reset must not depict the person falling.
  • Story fidelity: Preserve all story events and their order. No extra characters, dialogue, explanations, or additional magical consequences.

Claude Opus 5.5

Step 1 · Story

Golden Hour Loop — a short, cool piece for Instagram

A city rooftop at the very end of the day. The sun is sinking between two glass towers, and everything it touches turns to honey: the concrete ledge, a row of potted plants, the rippled surface of a small rooftop pool. The air is warm and hazy. Far below, traffic sounds blur into a soft hum, and a laid-back beat with a deep bassline rolls underneath it all.

A young woman in a loose white shirt and sunglasses sits on the ledge with her legs crossed, a cold drink sweating in her hand. The breeze lifts her hair. She tilts her face up toward the last of the sun and closes her eyes. Nothing is urgent here.

Then the world begins to play. The pool's reflection of the skyline shimmers and starts to ripple upward into the sky, as if the city were melting into liquid gold. Ice cubes in her glass catch the light and scatter it into tiny rainbows across the wall. Birds sweep across the sky in a wide arc, and their flight path leaves a faint trail of light that fades a second later. Every movement lands on the beat.

The sun touches the horizon. For one moment the whole scene glows almost too bright — pure gold, every edge burning. The woman lowers her sunglasses, looks straight out with a small, knowing smile, and raises her glass.

The light dips. The sky turns violet, the first windows in the towers flicker on, and the city lights come up one by one in rhythm with the music. The final frame melts seamlessly back into the opening sunset, so the whole piece loops forever — an endless golden hour, made to be watched twice.

Step 2 · Operational chunks

Golden Hour Loop: Operational Chunks

Chunk 1: Rooftop at sunset

  • Duration: 3 s (0–3 s)
  • Visual: Wide, slow push-in on a city rooftop at sunset. The sun sinks between two glass towers. Honey-gold light on the concrete ledge, potted plants and the rippled surface of a small rooftop pool. Warm haze.
  • On-screen text: None
  • Audio: Laid-back beat with a deep bassline starts. Distant traffic blurs into a soft hum.
  • Entities:
  • Two glass towers framing the sun (same position in every chunk).
  • Rooftop: concrete ledge, row of potted plants, small pool.
  • Environment: City rooftop, golden hour. Warm honey and amber palette, soft haze.
  • Depends on: Chunk 5 end frame. The opening frame must match the final frame so the loop is seamless. Generate this frame first and use it as the anchor for both ends.
  • Source: "A city rooftop at the very end of the day. The sun is sinking between two glass towers, and everything it touches turns to honey: the concrete ledge, a row of potted plants, the rippled surface of a small rooftop pool. The air is warm and hazy. Far below, traffic sounds blur into a soft hum, and a laid-back beat with a deep bassline rolls underneath it all."

  • On-screen text: None
  • Audio: Beat continues. Soft breeze.
  • Entities:
  • Woman: young, loose white shirt, sunglasses, same face and hair throughout.
  • Drink: cold glass with ice cubes, condensation.
  • Environment: Same rooftop and golden light as Chunk 1.
  • Depends on: Chunk 1 (same rooftop, same sun position).
  • Source: "A young woman in a loose white shirt and sunglasses sits on the ledge with her legs crossed, a cold drink sweating in her hand. The breeze lifts her hair. She tilts her face up toward the last of the sun and closes her eyes. Nothing is urgent here."

  • Duration: 4 s (6–10 s), quick beat-synced cuts inside the chunk

  1. The pool's reflection of the skyline ripples upward into the sky, as if the city were melting into liquid gold.
  2. Close-up: ice cubes in her glass scatter tiny rainbows across the wall.
  3. Birds sweep across the sky in a wide arc, leaving a faint light trail that fades a second later.

  • Depends on: Chunk 2 (same glass, same woman, same light).
  • Source: "Then the world begins to play. The pool's reflection of the skyline shimmers and starts to ripple upward into the sky, as if the city were melting into liquid gold. Ice cubes in her glass catch the light and scatter it into tiny rainbows across the wall. Birds sweep across the sky in a wide arc, and their flight path leaves a faint trail of light that fades a second later. Every movement lands on the beat."

Chunk 4: Peak glow and the toast

  • Duration: 3 s (10–13 s)
  • Visual: The sun touches the horizon and the scene blooms almost too bright: pure gold, every edge burning. The woman lowers her sunglasses, looks straight into the camera with a small, knowing smile, and raises her glass.
  • On-screen text: None
  • Audio: Musical peak or accent on the glass raise.
  • Entities: Woman (same as Chunk 2, sunglasses lowered so her eyes are visible). Drink.
  • Environment: Rooftop, sun on the horizon, overexposed golden bloom.
  • Depends on: Chunk 2 (same woman, same clothes, same ledge position).
  • Source: "The sun touches the horizon. For one moment the whole scene glows almost too bright — pure gold, every edge burning. The woman lowers her sunglasses, looks straight out with a small, knowing smile, and raises her glass."

Chunk 5: Dusk and loop back

  • Duration: 2–3 s (13–15/16 s)
  • Visual: The light dips and the sky turns violet. Windows in the towers flicker on, and city lights come up one by one in rhythm with the beat. The last frame melts seamlessly back into the opening sunset frame of Chunk 1.
  • On-screen text: None
  • Audio: Beat continues. The music must also loop: the last bar flows back into the first bar with no gap or pop.
  • Entities: Towers, city lights, rooftop.
  • Environment: Dusk, violet sky, city lights, then a transition back to golden hour.
  • Depends on: Chunk 4 (continuous light change). Chunk 1 start frame (the final frame must match it for the loop).
  • Source: "The light dips. The sky turns violet, the first windows in the towers flicker on, and the city lights come up one by one in rhythm with the music. The final frame melts seamlessly back into the opening sunset, so the whole piece loops forever — an endless golden hour, made to be watched twice."
Global requirements
  • Platform: Instagram ("for my insta"). Deliver as a Reel: 9:16 vertical, 1080×1920. Keep key action in the center safe zone, away from the UI overlays.
  • Total duration: not specified. Target ~15 s, which is short enough to rewatch and loop.
  • Seamless loop: the last frame and the last audio bar must flow into the first frame and first bar with no visible or audible seam.
  • Identity: the woman (loose white shirt, sunglasses, same face and hair) and the rooftop layout (ledge, plants, pool, two towers) stay consistent in every chunk.
  • Music: one laid-back beat with a deep bassline throughout. Cut edits and effects to the beat. Use licensed or original music suitable for Instagram. No narration, no dialogue.
  • Style: "cool": dreamy, cinematic, slightly surreal. Warm honey-gold grade shifting to violet at the end. Soft haze, gentle film grain.
  • On-screen text: none.
  • Open choice: the request ("something cool") gives no subject, brand or account details. If the user has a niche, brand or face they want featured, the concept should change to fit it.

4. Director's shooting script

SHOOTING SCRIPT — “SWITCH THE VIBE”

Format: Instagram Reel · 9:16 vertical · 24 seconds Concept: An ordinary moment flips into a cinematic entrance with one finger snap. Cast/location: One person, one quiet street or textured wall. Shoot on a phone. Visual style: Muted opening → warm, high-contrast reveal → confident, clean finish. Music: A cleared electronic beat with a bass drop at 6 seconds.

No footage was uploaded, so this is an original concept you can shoot.

SCENE 1 — AUTOPILOT

Timing: 0–6 seconds

SHOT 1 — THE ORDINARY MOMENT

0:00–0:03 | 3 seconds

  • Shot type: Medium shot, waist up.
  • Camera movement: Slow push toward the subject.
  • Action: Subject stands against a plain wall, scrolling on their phone. They pause and look directly into the lens.
  • On-screen text: “SAME DAY.” Small white uppercase text, centered.
  • Dialogue/narration: None.
  • Sound/music: Quiet street ambience. A filtered electronic pulse begins.
  • Direction: Keep the pose casual and the colors slightly desaturated.
SHOT 2 — THE SWITCH

0:03–0:06 | 3 seconds

  • Shot type: Extreme close-up of the subject’s hand.
  • Camera movement: Quick push-in, stopping just before the snap.
  • Action: Subject raises their hand and snaps their fingers. Cut precisely as the fingers connect.
  • On-screen text: “DIFFERENT ENERGY.” Appears at 4 seconds.
  • Dialogue/narration: None.
  • Sound/music: The music briefly drops out. An exaggerated finger snap lands at 6 seconds.
SCENE 2 — MAIN CHARACTER MODE

Timing: 6–18 seconds

SHOT 3 — THE REVEAL

0:06–0:09 | 3 seconds

  • Shot type: Medium shot matching Shot 1’s framing.
  • Camera movement: Fast push-in.
  • Action: Match cut to the same subject in the same position, now wearing a jacket and sunglasses. They pocket their phone and lift their chin.
  • On-screen text: “SWITCH ON.” Flashes for the first half-second.
  • Dialogue/narration: None.
  • Sound/music: Bass drop. Full drums enter with a sharp impact.
  • Direction: Switch to warm color grading and stronger contrast. Use sunlight from the side.
SHOT 4 — THE DETAIL

0:09–0:12 | 3 seconds

  • Shot type: Extreme close-up of sunglasses and eyes.
  • Camera movement: Short lateral slide.
  • Action: Subject adjusts their sunglasses, then looks past the camera.
  • On-screen text: None.
  • Dialogue/narration: None.
  • Sound/music: Beat continues. Add a subtle click as the glasses settle.
  • Direction: Catch a clean reflection in the lenses; avoid showing the camera.
SHOT 5 — THE FIRST STEP

0:12–0:15 | 3 seconds

  • Shot type: Ground-level close-up of shoes.
  • Camera movement: Low tracking move alongside the feet.
  • Action: One deliberate step lands on the beat, followed by two confident walking steps.
  • On-screen text: “MOVE LIKE YOU MEAN IT.”
  • Dialogue/narration: None.
  • Sound/music: Footsteps layered into the percussion.
  • Direction: Film at 60 fps for slight slow motion. Keep the camera operator out of the walking path.
SHOT 6 — THE WALK

0:15–0:18 | 3 seconds

  • Shot type: Medium-full shot from a slightly low angle.
  • Camera movement: Track backward as the subject approaches.
  • Action: Subject walks toward the lens. Their jacket moves naturally; they give a brief, understated smile.
  • On-screen text: None.
  • Dialogue/narration: None.
  • Sound/music: The track reaches its strongest phrase. Add a light transition whoosh at the cut.
  • Direction: Confident, not exaggerated. Use a spotter for the backward camera move.
SCENE 3 — OWN THE FRAME

Timing: 18–24 seconds

SHOT 7 — THE HERO FRAME

0:18–0:21 | 3 seconds

  • Shot type: Wide shot with the subject centered.
  • Camera movement: Gentle half-orbit around the subject.
  • Action: Subject stops in a patch of sunlight and turns toward the camera.
  • On-screen text: “MAKE YOUR OWN MOMENT.” Large, clean type within Instagram’s safe area.
  • Dialogue/narration: None.
  • Sound/music: Bass holds while a bright synth accent lifts the moment.
  • Direction: Leave space around the subject. Let the location feel expansive.
SHOT 8 — THE LOOP

0:21–0:24 | 3 seconds

  • Shot type: Close-up.
  • Camera movement: Static.
  • Action: Subject leans toward the camera and covers the lens with their palm. The frame becomes fully dark by 23.5 seconds.
  • On-screen text: Main text disappears as the hand approaches. Optional small @yourhandle before the lens is covered.
  • Dialogue/narration: None.
  • Sound/music: Soft palm-contact sound. Resolve the beat into the opening’s filtered pulse.
  • Direction: Begin Shot 1 with a few dark frames so the ending loops smoothly back to the start.
EDITING NOTES
  • Cut on the beat; make the 6-second snap transition the standout moment.
  • Keep text away from the bottom caption area and right-side interface icons.
  • Favor clean match cuts over heavy effects.
  • Export at 1080 × 1920, with no letterboxing.

"Golden Hour Loop" — Instagram Reel Shooting Script

You didn't specify a subject, so I've written a short, visually striking reel that works for almost any account: a seamless-loop "one day in 15 seconds" piece built around light and match cuts. If you tell me what your account is about (food, travel, fitness, a product, your business), I can rewrite it around that.

Runtime: 15 seconds, designed to loop seamlessly Format: 9:16 vertical, 1080 × 1920. Keep key action in the center; leave the bottom 20% clear for the caption and UI. Look: Warm, cinematic, slightly dreamy. Golden light, soft lens flares, rich color. Music: Upbeat lo-fi / chill-house track at about 110 BPM, with cuts landing on the beat. Choose a trending audio from Instagram's library at upload if you want more reach.

  • Dialogue / narration: None
  • Sound: Whoosh on the whip pan.

Each shot cuts on the beat. Use match cuts: each shot's shape or motion carries into the next.

  • Sound: Music builds into the chorus.
Shot 8 — Close-up, speed ramp
  • Timing: 10.0 – 12.0 s
  • Action: Silhouette walking against the setting sun, hair and clothes moving in the breeze. Speed ramps from normal to slow motion.
  • Camera: Static, low angle, sun directly behind the subject.
  • Sound: Music peaks.

  • Timing: 12.0 – 15.0 s

5. Story, then change-based chunks

Step 2 · Change-based chunks (step 1 is the story from approach 3)

Baseline — first-frame state

Format: 9:16 vertical, 1080 × 1920, 24 fps. Total duration: 49 seconds. Designed as a visually striking Instagram video.

Style: Cinematic photorealism with seamless magical-realism effects, tactile wet surfaces, restrained film grain and soft highlight bloom. No cartoon rendering.

Character: One young adult with medium-brown skin and short, tousled dark hair. Their appearance remains consistent throughout. Oversized charcoal jacket, plain black shirt, dark trousers, spotless white sneakers, initially with two white shoelaces. They begin just inside the corner-store doorway, holding a plain kraft-paper cup in their left hand. Their expression is neutral.

Products and objects:

  • One unbranded paper cup; its appearance remains unchanged.
  • An ordinary shallow puddle beside the curb.
  • A flickering pink corner-store sign reading “OPEN,” reflected in the puddle.
  • Closed shop shutters, unpainted brick walls and a pedestrian crossing with flat white stripes.
  • Rooftops with chimneys and hanging laundry. Initially, the laundry is still and non-glowing.
  • No fish, luminous ripples, flower murals, floating stairs, drifting constellations or sparks.

Environment: A nearly deserted city street. The tiny corner store is the only visibly active business; neighboring shops have their shutters down. Rain-wet pavement and asphalt glisten. No other people or moving vehicles.

Time and lighting: Late night throughout; no dawn transition. Cool-white fluorescent light spills from the store. Dim street lighting and the flickering pink sign illuminate the wet surfaces.

Palette: Charcoal, slate, muted brick, cool white and localized pink reflections.

Camera: Stationary, street-level wide establishing shot. The street appears empty because the character is still inside the doorway.

Audio: Quiet nighttime city ambience, fluorescent hum, faint sign buzz and a subdued instrumental electronic beat. No dialogue or vocals.

On-screen text: Only the diegetic “OPEN” sign; no captions, logos or added overlays.

Continuity rule: Unlisted properties persist, including off-screen objects. The shoelaces change during the reset, before the homeward walk, but their colors are not clearly revealed until the final close-up.

Chunks

  • Quote: "A city rooftop at the very end of the day.   • Changes: sun: sinking slowly between the towers; pool surface: gently rippling.
  • Type: continuous
  • Duration: ~2 s
  • On-screen text and audio: traffic hum and beat start.

  • Quote: "A young woman in a loose white shirt and sunglasses sits on the ledge with her legs crossed, a cold drink sweating in her hand. The breeze lifts her hair. She tilts her face up toward the last of the sun and closes her eyes. Nothing is urgent here."

  • Changes:

  • pool reflection: still reflection → shimmering, rippling upward into the sky as liquid gold

  • Signpost: "The sun touches the horizon"

  • sun: above the horizon → touching the horizon
  • exposure: warm → almost overexposed, pure gold, edges burning
  • woman: eyes closed, face up → sunglasses lowered, looking straight into camera, small knowing smile, glass raised

  • Duration: ~3 s
  • Depends on: Chunk 3

Chunk 5 — Dusk, and back to the start

  • Quote: "The light dips. The sky turns violet, the first windows in the towers flicker on, and the city lights come up one by one in rhythm with the music. The final frame melts seamlessly back into the opening sunset, so the whole piece loops forever — an endless golden hour, made to be watched twice."
  • Signpost: "The light dips"

  • lighting: blazing gold → dipped
  • sky: gold → violet
  • tower windows: dark → first windows flicker on
  • city lights: off → coming up one by one, in rhythm with the music

  • Type: continuous (morph/dissolve into the opening frame)
  • Duration: ~3 s
  • On-screen text and audio: beat must end on a bar that loops cleanly back to the start of the track.
  • Depends on: Chunk 4; end state must equal the baseline (Chunk 1 start)

A warm, festive room glows with soft golden light. Balloons and a “60” birthday banner surround a table holding a cake topped with two candles shaped like a six and a zero.

Dad stands beside the cake, the unmistakable star of this 20-second birthday celebration. He notices the decorations, breaks into a smile, and gives a small, playful bow as loved ones cheer around him. The mood is affectionate and joyful—a celebration of him, not just the milestone.

He leans forward and blows out the candles. As the little wisps of smoke rise, he laughs and accepts a warm hug.

The celebration ends with Dad smiling beneath the birthday banner and a simple message appearing:

“Happy 60th Birthday, Dad! You turn 60 this Saturday—and we can’t wait to celebrate you.”

Claude Opus 5.5

Step 1 · Story

Sixty — a 20-second birthday video for Dad

A warm Saturday morning. Sunlight spills across a backyard patio strung with paper lanterns and a hand-painted banner that reads Happy 60th, Dad! A long table is set with mismatched plates, a jug of lemonade, and a cake in the center, its frosting piped with a big candle-shaped 60. Upbeat, gently nostalgic music plays — something with acoustic guitar and a little swing.

Dad walks out through the back door, still holding his coffee mug. He stops. His eyebrows go up. A surprised laugh breaks out of him, and he shakes his head the way dads do when they are pretending not to be moved.

Memories drift in around him like old photographs coming to life: Dad younger, with darker hair, holding a baby in a hospital blanket; Dad teaching a kid to ride a bike down a quiet street, running behind with one hand on the seat; Dad at the grill on a summer evening, waving a spatula like a conductor's baton. Each moment glows for a beat and then softens back into the present.

Back on the patio, Dad leans over the cake. The candles flicker. He takes a big breath, cheeks puffed, and blows them all out in one go. Smoke curls upward and he grins, proud of himself. Confetti bursts into the air and drifts down around his shoulders.

He raises his mug like a toast, looks right at us, and smiles — a little teary, very happy.

The final words appear across the warm sunlight:

Happy 60th Birthday, Dad!

The video ends at twenty seconds.

Note: to put your dad himself in the video, please upload one or more clear photos of him — ideally a recent front-facing photo, plus any older photos you would like used for the memory moments. You can also tell me his name or a nickname if you want it on the banner and the final card instead of "Dad."

  • Depends on: None
  • Source: "A warm Saturday morning. Sunlight spills across a backyard patio strung with paper lanterns and a hand-painted banner that reads Happy 60th, Dad! A long table is set with mismatched plates, a jug of lemonade, and a cake in the center, its frosting piped with a big candle-shaped 60. Upbeat, gently nostalgic music plays — something with acoustic guitar and a little swing."

Chunk 2: Dad walks out

  • Duration: 3 s (3–6 s)
  • Visual: Dad steps out through the back door holding a coffee mug. He stops, his eyebrows go up, and he laughs in surprise. He shakes his head the way dads do when they are pretending not to be moved.
  • On-screen text: None
  • Audio: Music continues. Natural laugh (optional, no spoken lines needed).
  • Entities:
  • Dad: identity locked to the uploaded photo(s). Age 60. Same face, hair and build in every present-day chunk. Outfit chosen here (e.g. casual shirt) stays the same in Chunks 2, 4 and 5.
  • Coffee mug: same mug in Chunk 5.
  • Environment: Same patio and morning light as Chunk 1.
  • Depends on: Chunk 1 (same patio set). Uploaded photo of Dad.
  • Source: "Dad walks out through the back door, still holding his coffee mug. He stops. His eyebrows go up. A surprised laugh breaks out of him, and he shakes his head the way dads do when they are pretending not to be moved."

Chunk 3: Memories

  • Duration: 6 s (6–12 s), three memory beats of ~2 s each
  • Visual: Memories drift in like old photographs coming to life. Each glows for a beat, then softens back into the present:
  1. Younger Dad with darker hair holds a baby in a hospital blanket.
  2. Dad runs behind a kid on a bike down a quiet street, one hand on the seat.
  3. Dad at the grill on a summer evening, waving a spatula like a conductor's baton.
  • On-screen text: None
  • Audio: Music continues, slightly softer and more nostalgic.
  • Entities: Younger Dad (must be recognizably the same man as present-day Dad). Use the user's older photos if uploaded. Otherwise, de-age from the recent photo. Baby, kid on bike, grill and spatula.
  • Environment: Hospital room, quiet street, summer-evening backyard. Vintage photo look: warm grade, soft edges, light film grain, photo-print borders or glow.
  • Depends on: Chunk 2 (transition out of and back into the patio). Dad's identity from the uploaded photo(s).
  • Source: "Memories drift in around him like old photographs coming to life: Dad younger, with darker hair, holding a baby in a hospital blanket; Dad teaching a kid to ride a bike down a quiet street, running behind with one hand on the seat; Dad at the grill on a summer evening, waving a spatula like a conductor's baton. Each moment glows for a beat and then softens back into the present."

Chunk 4: Blowing out the candles

  • Duration: 4 s (12–16 s)
  • Visual: Back on the patio. Dad leans over the cake, the candles flicker, he puffs his cheeks and blows them all out in one go. Smoke curls up and he grins. Confetti bursts into the air and drifts down around his shoulders.
  • On-screen text: None (the "60" on the cake stays visible).
  • Audio: Music lifts. Blow, small cheer or confetti pop.
  • Entities: Dad (as in Chunk 2). Cake (as in Chunk 1). Confetti.
  • Environment: Same patio and morning light.
  • Depends on: Chunk 1 (same cake and table). Chunk 2 (same Dad, same outfit).
  • Source: "Back on the patio, Dad leans over the cake. The candles flicker. He takes a big breath, cheeks puffed, and blows them all out in one go. Smoke curls upward and he grins, proud of himself. Confetti bursts into the air and drifts down around his shoulders."

Chunk 5: Toast and final card

  • Duration: 4 s (16–20 s)
  • Visual: Dad raises his mug like a toast, looks straight into the camera, and smiles, a little teary and very happy. Final words appear over the warm sunlight and hold to the end.
  • On-screen text (exact): Happy 60th Birthday, Dad! (or with his name or nickname if given). Add as an editor text overlay, not generated in the image.
  • Audio: Music resolves and ends at 20 s.
  • Entities: Dad (as in Chunk 2). Coffee mug (as in Chunk 2). Confetti remnants on his shoulders.
  • Environment: Same patio, warm sunlight.
  • Depends on: Chunk 4 end frame (continuous moment, confetti still on his shoulders). Chunk 2 (same mug).
  • Source: "He raises his mug like a toast, looks right at us, and smiles — a little teary, very happy. The final words appear across the warm sunlight: Happy 60th Birthday, Dad! The video ends at twenty seconds."
Global requirements
  • Total duration: exactly 20 seconds.
  • Dad must be in it: Dad must be the real person from the user's photos, recognizable in every chunk he appears in (Chunks 2–5), including the younger version in the memories. Request photos before production.
  • Occasion facts: birthday video for Dad turning 60, on Saturday. All "60" text must be correct.
  • Exact text: banner Happy 60th, Dad!. Final card Happy 60th Birthday, Dad!. Add all text as overlays and check the spelling before delivery. Swap "Dad" for his name only if the user asks.
  • Delivery timing: the video must be finished and delivered before Saturday.
  • Aspect ratio: not specified. Ask the user, or default to 16:9. Offer 9:16 for phone sharing (WhatsApp, Instagram Stories).
  • Music: one continuous upbeat, gently nostalgic track (acoustic guitar, a little swing) across all 20 s. No narration or dialogue.
  • Style: warm, sunny, heartfelt, realistic. Memory beats use a vintage photograph look, then return to the warm present.
  • Optional inputs to ask for: Dad's name or nickname, older photos for the memories, and any real family details (kids' names, his hobbies) to make the memory beats personal.

4. Director's shooting script

GPT-6.1 Sol

“60 Years, One Amazing Dad”

Runtime: Exactly 20 seconds Tone: Warm, joyful, personal Music: Light, upbeat acoustic guitar, softened beneath speech.

Production note: Film your dad for the shots below, since no footage was uploaded. Use natural light and a simple birthday cake with “60” candles.

Scene 1 — The Birthday Star

Timing: 0–8 seconds

Shot 1 — 0–4 seconds | 4 seconds
  • Shot type: Medium shot of Dad sitting in his favorite chair or standing in the garden.
  • Camera movement: Gentle push-in.
  • Action: Dad looks toward the camera and smiles naturally.
  • On-screen text: “This Saturday…”
  • Narration: “This Saturday, we celebrate someone pretty special.”
  • Sound and music: Acoustic music begins softly; subtle room or garden ambience.
Shot 2 — 4–8 seconds | 4 seconds
  • Shot type: Close-up of Dad’s face.
  • Camera movement: Static.
  • Action: Capture a genuine laugh—have someone off-camera tell a familiar family joke.
  • On-screen text: “60 years young.”
  • Narration: “Sixty years young. Still one of a kind.”
  • Sound and music: Let Dad’s laughter come through beneath the narration; music continues.
Scene 2 — Love and Celebration

Timing: 8–16 seconds

Shot 3 — 8–12 seconds | 4 seconds
  • Shot type: Medium-wide shot of Dad with you or other family members.
  • Camera movement: Gentle handheld movement.
  • Action: Give Dad a hug; he remains clearly visible at the center of the frame.
  • On-screen text: “Thanks for everything, Dad.”
  • Narration: “Thanks for the laughs, the love, and everything.”
  • Sound and music: Natural laughter and clothing rustle; music builds slightly.
Shot 4 — 12–16 seconds | 4 seconds
  • Shot type: Medium close-up, with the birthday cake in the foreground and Dad behind it.
  • Camera movement: Small tilt from the “60” candles up to Dad.
  • Action: Dad leans forward and blows out the candles.
  • On-screen text: “Make a wish!”
  • Dialogue, off-camera: “Make a wish, Dad!”
  • Sound and music: Candle-blowing sound followed by a brief cheer; music stays beneath the voices.
Scene 3 — The Birthday Message

Timing: 16–20 seconds

Shot 5 — 16–20 seconds | 4 seconds
  • Shot type: Medium shot of Dad smiling, with family gathered beside him.
  • Camera movement: Static; hold the composition through the final frame.
  • Action: Everyone leans in toward Dad. He smiles or waves at the camera.
  • On-screen text:

“Happy 60th Birthday, Dad!” “We love you ♥”

  • Dialogue, together: “Happy sixtieth birthday! We love you!”
  • Sound and music: Warm cheers; music rises to a bright final chord and ends at exactly 20 seconds.

Claude Opus 5.5

"Sixty Looks Good on You" — 20-Second Birthday Video

Runtime: 20 seconds Format: 9:16 vertical (easy to send by text or post to stories); a 16:9 version works with the same shots Tone: Warm, celebratory, a little funny, a little heartfelt. Music: Upbeat, feel-good acoustic or soft-rock track at about 115 BPM, building to a big finish.

Before we produce this: You'd like your dad in the video, but no photos were uploaded. To put him in it, please share 3–6 photos of your dad: at least one clear, recent, front-facing photo of his face, plus a few older photos (childhood, young adult, with family) if you have them. I've written the script around those photos. Every shot marked [DAD PHOTO] needs one. Also helpful: his name or what you call him ("Dad," "Pops"), and a short personal message from you.
SCENE 1 — THE COUNTDOWN (0.0 – 4.0 s)
Shot 1 — Close-up, slow push-in
  • Timing: 0.0 – 2.0 s
  • Action: A birthday cake on a table in a warm, softly lit room. Candles shaped as the numbers 6 and 0 are lit; flames flicker.
  • Camera: Slow push-in toward the candles.
  • On-screen text: None
  • Dialogue / narration: None
  • Sound: Match strike and soft crackle; music begins with a gentle guitar intro.
Shot 2 — Extreme close-up, static
  • Timing: 2.0 – 4.0 s
  • Action: The "60" candles fill the frame.
  • Camera: Static.
  • On-screen text: This Saturday, someone special turns… (white, center, fades in at 2.2 s)
  • Dialogue / narration: None
  • Sound: Music builds with a rising drum roll.
SCENE 2 — THROUGH THE YEARS (4.0 – 12.0 s)

A quick photo montage, each cut on the beat. Each photo has a gentle Ken Burns move so the stills feel alive.

Shot 3 — [DAD PHOTO: childhood], slow push-in
  • Timing: 4.0 – 6.0 s
  • Action: Your dad as a child. Subtle warm vintage grade.
  • Camera: Slow push-in on his face.
  • On-screen text: The early years (small, lower third)
  • Sound: Music kicks in fully on the cut at 4.0 s.
Shot 4 — [DAD PHOTO: young adult], slow pan left to right
  • Timing: 6.0 – 8.0 s
  • Action: Your dad as a young man.
  • Camera: Slow pan across the photo.
  • On-screen text: The legend begins (small, lower third)
  • Sound: Music continues.
Shot 5 — [DAD PHOTO: with family / with you], slow pull-back
  • Timing: 8.0 – 10.0 s
  • Action: Your dad with the family or with you.
  • Camera: Slow pull-back revealing the whole photo.
  • On-screen text: Best Dad ever (small, lower third)
  • Sound: Music lifts.
Shot 6 — [DAD PHOTO: recent, front-facing], gentle animation
  • Timing: 10.0 – 12.0 s
  • Action: Your dad today. The photo is subtly animated: he breaks into a warm smile and gives a small nod or a thumbs-up. His face, features, and age stay exactly as in the photo.
  • Camera: Static with a slight push-in.
  • On-screen text: None
  • Sound: Light "ding" accent as he smiles.
SCENE 3 — THE BIG REVEAL (12.0 – 20.0 s)
Shot 7 — Medium, static, with confetti burst
  • Timing: 12.0 – 16.0 s
  • Action: Return to the cake from Shot 1. A burst of gold and blue confetti falls across the frame. The candles blow out on their own with a puff of smoke.
  • Camera: Static, then a small "bump" zoom on the confetti burst at 12.0 s.
  • On-screen text: HAPPY 60th BIRTHDAY, DAD! (big, bold, gold, center, pops in at 12.2 s with a slight bounce)
  • Dialogue / narration: None
  • Sound: Party popper and a cheer sound effect; music hits its big chorus.
Shot 8 — [DAD PHOTO: recent] as closing card, slow push-in
  • Timing: 16.0 – 20.0 s
  • Action: Your dad's recent photo inside a soft gold frame, with gently falling confetti.
  • Camera: Slow push-in.
  • On-screen text: We love you! ❤️ (center-lower, fades in at 16.5 s)

Optional second line: — [your name / the family]

  • Dialogue / narration (optional): A short recorded voice message from you, about 3 seconds, e.g. "Happy sixtieth, Dad. Love you." Record it on your phone and send it with the photos.
  • Sound: Music resolves on a final warm chord, ringing out to 20.0 s.
What I need from you to finish this
  1. Photos of your dad (required): one clear recent front-facing photo, plus optional childhood, young-adult, and family photos. If you only have recent photos, Scene 2 can become three recent favorites instead.
  2. What to call him in the text (default: "Dad").
  3. Who it's from for the sign-off line (optional).
  4. A voice message (optional).
  5. Any inside joke or favorite thing of his (golf, fishing, his car, his famous BBQ) that I can work into one of the captions.

Step 2 · Change-based chunks (step 1 is the story from approach 3)

Baseline — first-frame state
  • Duration: 20 seconds total.
  • Aspect ratio: 16:9.
  • Style: Warm, realistic live-action birthday tribute; affectionate and joyful, with Dad as the focus.
  • Dad: The user’s father, turning 60 this Saturday. He stands upright beside the cake, with a relaxed expression and his arms at his sides. His identity, facial features, hair, build, and wardrobe must remain consistent throughout. No uploads were supplied, so his actual likeness requires a supplied or confirmed reference; it must not be invented as though verified.
  • Loved ones: A small group stands around Dad and the table. One is close enough to hug him later. Their identities, appearances, and clothing remain consistent.
  • Objects: A table holds one birthday cake with two lit numeral candles: a six and a zero. Balloons decorate the room. A physical banner displaying “60” hangs on the rear wall. These objects retain their designs and positions unless explicitly changed below. No branded products.
  • Environment: One warm, festive indoor room.
  • Time of day: Evening interior, chosen as a production default; no calendar date is shown.
  • Lighting: Soft golden room light, with a small additional glow from the candle flames.
  • Palette: Warm gold, cream, and muted festive accents.
  • Camera: Steady, medium-wide shot showing Dad, the cake, nearby loved ones, balloons, and banner.
  • Audio: Quiet, warm instrumental music and subtle room ambience. No narration or cheering yet.
  • On-screen text: None beyond the physical “60” banner.

A warm, festive room glows with soft golden light. Balloons and a “60” birthday banner surround a table holding a cake topped with two candles shaped like a six and a zero.

Dad stands beside the cake, the unmistakable star of this 20-second birthday celebration. He notices the decorations, breaks into a smile, and gives a small, playful bow as loved ones cheer around him. The mood is affectionate and joyful—a celebration of him, not just the milestone.

He leans forward and blows out the candles.

As the little wisps of smoke rise, he laughs and accepts a warm hug.

The celebration ends with Dad smiling beneath the birthday banner and a simple message appearing:

  • Dad’s expression: laughing → smiling.
  • Interaction with the nearby loved one: embracing → hug released.
  • Candle smoke: rising wisps → dissipated.

“Happy 60th Birthday, Dad! You turn 60 this Saturday—and we can’t wait to celebrate you.”

  • Cheering and laughter soften and stop.
